[![remio: Your Personal ChatGPT](https://media.theresanaiforthat.com/icons/remio.png?width=100)](https://theresanaiforthat.com/ai/remio/)

[remio: Your Personal ChatGPT](https://theresanaiforthat.com/ai/remio/)v3.8Version updatev3.8Jun 26, 2026
Most AI tools start from zero. You paste in context, re-explain your work, and hope the output is relevant. Every. Single. Time.

remio is different.

It automatically captures everything you work on — web pages you browse, documents you read, meetings you record, emails you send — and turns it all into a living, private knowledge base. No manual tagging. No copy-pasting. No setup.

Then, when you need to get something done, remio already knows your context. Write a doc, draft an email, generate a report, create slides — just tell it what you need. It pulls from your actual work history to produce output that's genuinely relevant to you.

What makes remio different:

🧠 Passive, automatic context capture — works silently in the background

🔒 Local-first & privacy-focused — your data stays on your device

⚡ BYOK support — bring your own API key from OpenAI, Google, DeepSeek and more

🖥️ Local model support via Ollama

📎 Connects to Gmail, Google Docs, local files, YouTube, and more

🛠️ Build reusable aApp workflows powered by your own knowledge

This is not another note-taking app. This is your personal agent — one that already knows your world.

From knowledge to getting things done.

👉 Try remio today.

[Get Tailored Answer with Your Personal ChatGPT](https://theresanaiforthat.com/ai/remio/)

[BeatMV](https://theresanaiforthat.com/ai/beatmv/)v2.6Version updatev2.6Jun 25, 2026
🎬 BeatMV v2.6 — Product Update

Turn any Suno or Udio song into a karaoke-ready lyric video — lyrics that hit every beat, baked right into the export.

✨ New Features

🎤 Synced Lyrics for Suno & Udio Paste a Suno or Udio song and BeatMV reads its word-level timestamps to lay out perfectly timed, line-by-line lyrics across your video. Uploaded or external tracks get AI-estimated timing automatically — no manual syncing.

🎬 Cinematic Subtitle Styles Style your lyrics like a real music video: 20+ display fonts (Anton, Bebas, Oswald, Montserrat, plus CJK), three sizes, color presets, top/center/bottom positioning, and smooth fade in/out — on by default.

📤 One-Click Lyric Video Export Lyrics burn directly into the final MP4 from the export dialog, so what you preview is exactly what you share — ready for TikTok, Reels, and Shorts.

🔧 Improvements & Fixes

🔁 Auto-Retry for Stuck Scenes Scenes that fail or stall (timeouts, page reloads) now retry automatically in the background — and you can hit Cancel on any retrying card to stop the loop instantly.

🎵 Smoother Suno / Udio Import Refined CDN extraction for cover art, audio, and metadata so imports land cleanly the first time.

[Turn any song into scroll-stopping videos in minutes with AI-directed scripts and](https://theresanaiforthat.com/ai/beatmv/)

[![AirMusic - AI Music Video Generator](https://media.theresanaiforthat.com/icons/airmusic-ai-music-video-generator.svg?width=100)](https://theresanaiforthat.com/ai/airmusic-ai-music-video-generator/)

[AirMusic - AI Music Video Generator](https://theresanaiforthat.com/ai/airmusic-ai-music-video-generator/)v2.5Version updatev2.5Jun 25, 2026
🚀 AirMusic AI Music Video Generator v2.5

23% Lower Video Cost, Mobile Optimization, and Smarter Generation Control

AirMusic AI Music Video Generator v2.5 focuses on making AI music video creation more affordable, easier to use, and more reliable across devices.

This update reduces the credit cost of music video generation, improves the mobile creation experience, refines the song upload and video style selection flow, and fixes reported video stuttering issues.

📌 Summary

· AI music video generation now costs 23% fewer credits for the same video duration

· Mobile-optimized workflow for creating music videos from phones

· Improved song upload experience

· Smoother video style selection flow

· Cancel & regenerate support for slow image or video generations

· No credits charged for cancelled or failed tasks

· Fixes for reported video stuttering issues

· AirMusic also adds 25+ AI music styles, making it easier to create songs before turning them into videos

💰 AI Music Video Cost Reduced by 23%

AirMusic v2.5 optimizes the AI music video generation pipeline.

Creating a video of the same duration now requires 23% fewer credits. This helps users create more music videos with the same credit balance, especially when testing different scenes, video styles, or versions of the same song.

For creators who regularly make music videos for YouTube, TikTok, Instagram Reels, Shorts, or music promotion, this lowers the cost of repeated production.

📱 Mobile-Optimized AI Music Video Creation

The AI Music Video Generator is now better adapted for mobile devices.

Users can upload songs, choose video styles, adjust settings, and start generation more comfortably from a phone. This is useful for creators who produce and publish short-form music content directly from mobile workflows.

🎵 Improved Song Upload Flow

The song upload experience has been refined to reduce friction at the first step of video creation.

Users can move from uploading a track to choosing a video style more smoothly, whether they are using an original song, a Suno-generated track, or music created with AirMusic’s AI Music Generator.

🎨 Better Video Style Selection

AirMusic v2.5 improves the video style selection experience.

Users can choose the visual direction of the music video more clearly before generation starts. This helps when creating different types of videos, such as storytelling MVs, singing avatar videos, dance videos, lyric-style visuals, or social media clips.

⚡ Cancel & Regenerate Without Losing Credits

If image or video generation is too slow, users can now cancel and regenerate without extra credit loss.

This gives creators more control when testing different prompts, models, scenes, and visual styles. Instead of waiting for a slow generation to finish, users can restart the task and continue iterating.

Cancelled or failed tasks do not consume additional credits.

🎬 Fixes for Video Stuttering Issues

Based on user feedback, AirMusic v2.5 fixes a small number of reported video stuttering issues.

This improves playback smoothness and helps exported music videos feel more stable, especially for social media publishing and music promotion.

🎶 25+ Music Styles for Better Music-to-Video Workflows

AirMusic v2.5 also expands the AI Music Generator with 25+ music styles, including Pop, R&B, K-Pop, Hip Hop, Trap, Lo-Fi, House, Synthwave, Rock, Reggaeton, Afrobeats, Country, Jazz, Classical, Cinematic, and more.

This helps users create music in a clearer style direction before turning it into an AI-generated music video.

🎯 Best For

· Suno and AI music creators who want to turn generated songs into music videos with lower credit cost

· YouTube, TikTok, Instagram Reels, and Shorts creators who need a mobile-friendly workflow for music-based videos

· Artists and indie musicians who want to create promotional music videos without manual editing

· High-volume creators and agencies who need lower per-video cost for repeated content production

· Users testing multiple video styles who want to cancel slow generations and regenerate without wasting credits

· Creators who experienced video stuttering and need smoother playback in exported music videos

🚀🚀 25+ Music Styles, 23% Lower Video Credits Cost, and Smarter Generation Control 🚀🚀

AirMusic v2.5 adds 25+ music styles, reduces AI music video credits cost by 23%, improves music generation reliability with auto-retry, and introduces cancel & regenerate without credit deduction for slow or unwanted generations.

🎵 AI Music Generator — 25+ Styles

New supported styles:

Pop & Vocal: Pop, R&B, K-Pop, Gospel

Electronic: Electronic, House, Drum & Bass, Synthwave

Rock: Rock, Metal, Punk

🐦 Pounce v2.3 — Earn Credits & Track Your Wins

🎁 Introducing Referrals

1\. Earn credits for spreading the word; grab your personal referral link from the new Earn Credits page and share it anywhere.

2\. When a friend joins through your link and finishes setup, you both get 25 credits — no limit on how many people you invite.

3\. Make the link your own: customize it with your name or handle so it's memorable.

4\. Watch your impact roll in with live stats for visitors, signups, activated friends, and total credits earned.

📊 See Your Work, Beautifully

1\. A new activity calendar on your stats page shades every day by how busy Pounce was — spot your streaks at a glance and click any day to jump to its sessions.

2\. Past sessions are now a visual timeline: click any session to expand its full reply log and engagement breakdown.

3\. Every session card shows the campaign it ran and which accounts it acted as, so your history is finally easy to read.

🎨 A Cleaner Settings Experience

1\. Settings got a refreshed, more consistent layout that's easier to navigate.

2\. It now works noticeably better on mobile, so you can tweak your setup from anywhere.

💝 Free Credits, On Us

1\. Running low on the free plan? You'll now get a one-time gift of free credits to keep going.

2\. We'll send a friendly email nudge so you can come back and claim them whenever you're ready.

[There's An AI For That](https://theresanaiforthat.com/ai/there-s-an-ai-for-that/)v2.0Version updatev2.0Jun 19, 2026
There's An AI For That — v2 Release Changelog

The front page of AI.

TAAFT started as the place to find newly launched AI tools. With v2, it's now the place to track everything happening in AI: the tools, the companies building them, the models shipping, the money moving, and the news breaking. Here's what changed.

1\. Our homepage got a full revamp.

The homepage isn't just a tool directory anymore. It's a live view of the whole AI landscape. From one screen you can explore and filter across Tools, Companies, Countries, Tasks, Models, Papers, News, Robots, Devices, and the rest of the AI world. Whatever you're looking for, you can get to it straight from the front page.

2\. Rows instead of cards

We dropped the old three-column card grid for a clean row-based layout. It's more compact, and it shows a lot more at once. Pricing, task, release date, country, and views are all right there, so you can scan and compare without scrolling forever.

3\. More than a launch board

TAAFT used to be a feed where people launched tools. It still does that, but now there's a lot more underneath. You can dig into the companies behind the tools and filter them by Profitable, Unicorns, AI Native, sector, and more. It's a way to follow the players, not just their products.

4\. New sections

Companies — Filter every major AI company by valuation, valuation per employee, profitability, and sector.

Countries — See which countries lead in AI, who has the biggest market cap, and where investors are putting their money.

Tasks — Explore where AI is having the biggest impact, organized by what the tools actually do.

Models — Track new models as they drop, who built them, what they do, and whether they're open source.

News — Follow what's trending in AI right now, with topic tags, sources, and community voting.

Collections — Curated packs of the best AI tools for a specific job, built and shared by the community.

Fundraises — Keep up with the money moving in AI. See who just raised, the round, the size, the valuation, and the date, so you always know where the capital is flowing.

Investors — Browse the funds and companies backing AI. Sort by total invested, number of investments, type, and country, and see which big rounds they've been part of.

Repositories — Track the open source side of AI. See top repos by stars, forks, license, and recent activity, all tied back to the companies behind them.

Robots — Follow the hardware. Browse robots by company, category, country, availability, price, and release date, from humanoids to manipulators.

Devices — Keep an eye on AI hardware beyond robots: smart glasses, wearables, earbuds, speakers, and more, with availability, price, and what each one is actually for.

Papers — Dig into the research behind it all. Browse AI papers by company, with publish dates, authors, venues, and featured picks, plus a link straight to the source.

The short version

TAAFT isn't just where tools get launched anymore. It's where you keep up with all of AI in one place.

Welcome to v2.

[biela.dev](https://theresanaiforthat.com/ai/biela-dev/)v2.0.0Version updatev2.0.0Jun 19, 2026
Biela now builds full blockchain applications: smart contracts, tokens, marketplaces and more

- What's new\*

Biela has added full blockchain development to its platform. You can now build, test, and deploy smart contract-based applications without writing a single line of code, or ever touching a terminal.

The feature runs on EVM (Ethereum Virtual Machine) architecture, making it compatible with Ethereum and all major Layer 2 networks: Base, Arbitrum, Polygon, and others.

- --
- \*How it works, technically\*\*

When you describe a blockchain project in Biela, the AI generates both the smart contract and the full frontend application around it. From there, the workflow has three stages:

- \*Local testing\*\*

Biela spins up a local blockchain node automatically. Transactions are simulated, funds are fake, and nothing touches a real network. You test in a sandboxed environment without any configuration.

- \*Testnet (Sepolia)\*\*

When you're ready to share or test with others, Biela generates a crypto wallet for you and funds it with test ETH automatically. No prior wallet required. No faucet visits. No setup.

- \*Mainnet deployment\*\*

When you're ready to go live, you connect your own MetaMask wallet, sign the transaction, and deploy to whichever real EVM chain you choose. A few clicks.

The project dashboard is environment-aware: inside Biela it auto-connects to your local node; on a deployed version it shows a MetaMask connect button automatically.

- --
- \*What that means\*\*

→ \*\*You want to launch a token\*\*

describe it, Biela generates the contract and a full interface around it. Test it with fake funds first, then deploy to Ethereum when ready.

→ \*\*You want to build an NFT marketplace\*\*

describe the mechanics, Biela builds the smart contract and the marketplace UI together. No Solidity knowledge needed.

→ \*\*You have a startup idea that involves crypto payments or ownership\*\*

you can now build and ship an MVP of it without hiring a blockchain developer.

→ \*\*You want to experiment with Web3 but don't own crypto yet\*\*

Biela handles the test wallet and funds for you during development. You only need your own wallet at the very end, when going live.

→ \*\*You're a developer who wants to move faster\*\*

Biela abstracts the setup (local node, deployment scripts, wallet connection logic) so you skip configuration and go straight to building.

- --
- \*Why this is different\*\*

Most AI tools that touch blockchain help you write Solidity code; you still handle everything else yourself. Biela abstracts the entire pipeline: local environment, testnet infrastructure, wallet management, deployment, and frontend, in one flow. There's no equivalent of this in other no-code or vibecoding platforms today.

The blockchain feature is live now at biela.dev.

Build faster. Ship smarter. Create without limits.

[If you can imagine it, Biela can code it!](https://theresanaiforthat.com/ai/biela-dev/)

[LFM2.5-230M](https://theresanaiforthat.com/model/lfm2-5-230m/) [LFM2.5-230M is a 230M-parameter lightweight language model built on the LFM2 architecture, designed for fast inference across edge and cloud environments. Pre-trained on 19T tokens with a 32K context window, it targets tool use, data extraction, and agentic workflows. Runs on CPUs including Raspberry Pi 5 and mobile SoCs. Not recommended for math, code generation, or creative writing.](https://theresanaiforthat.com/model/lfm2-5-230m/)
